## 2026-10-16
* **Update**: [Scripts](manual/07-scripts.md), [options](manual/10-options.md), [the command line](manual/11-command-line.md) and [the deferred list](deferred.md): GitHub issue 27. Scripts are read and saved in the codepage of Options › Language (Windows-1250 for Polish; a module's `encoding.2da`, where it has one), as the game reads a compiled script's strings: a Polish module's scripts kept in Windows-1250 showed and saved as mojibake, read as Windows-1252 (no byte was lost: Windows-1252 carries every byte across, `Ź` too). Untouched lines save byte for byte, the compiled strings are the file's bytes, a character the codepage lacks is saved as `?` and said in the log, the toolbar names the codepage, and another language chosen reads again the scripts open without unsaved changes (those with some are saved as they were read). `mg lsp` reads scripts in the game's codepage (the user folder's `settings.tml`, or `--codepage`), and no longer hands the compiler a whole script as UTF-8 when one letter is outside Windows-1252.
* **Add**: [Areas](manual/04-areas.md): painting placeables (builders' asks on 1.31.0: El Grillo, dansen9191, Taro49, after varenx's toolset). **✏ Paint** on the area's toolbar (B) paints the placeables chosen in the palette (Ctrl + click chooses several, in the game's palette too) in a circle on the walkable ground: Shift + wheel sizes it; Density, Spacing, Flow, Turn (Q and E turn the brush), Scale and Height in a strip above the view, kept in the settings. A dab fills the circle up to its density, counting the chosen blueprints' placeables already there, and lays only on walkable faces; the next dab shows see-through before a press and is what a click lays; a stroke is one step, Paint Placeables. **Row** lays pieces end to end along a drag, each its model's length along the row with a Gap (Taro49 doubted the spacing could be automated: it comes from the model's box, and Gap makes up for what sticks out). Seen in pictures before and after (`area_paint` in `screens.rs`, `area_viewer_paints_placeables`, `area_viewer_lays_a_row_of_placeables`).
* **Update**: [The deferred list](deferred.md): what is about to be placed, see-through in an area's view, was taken for not drawn in the test harness's pictures (`area_ghost`'s statue was an empty box). It was drawn, blended as it should be: the shot put a dark stone statue half in a tower's wall, before a banner, at 0.6 opaque, where it all but vanished, in the harness and in the program alike (seen on the off-screen display). A blueprint about to be placed, a paste and the brush's dab are drawn 0.8 opaque (the tile brush's preview is 0.85), `area_ghost` looks at the statue on open cobbles, and a test holds the ghost to the view's picture, not only its counts (`a_blueprint_about_to_be_placed_shows_in_the_views_picture`). A copy in a wall or behind a building, still hidden as any model is, is in the deferred list.

The objects an area opens out to in the module tree are sorted by name, and waypoints are listed by tag, as Aurora lists them (the other on the row's tip); the Filter finds either. A click on an object's row leaves the tree where it is, so a second click makes the double click that opens its Properties (opening the area's tab scrolled its row, far above, to the middle), and an object selected in the area scrolls the tree only as far as its row needs, not to the area's row. A corpse placeable of CEP's lies dead in the area as in its Properties: a model without its state's animation (`default`) took its creature supermodel's `pause1`, and now shows its rest pose; seen in a picture before and after (`look_placeable_appearance`). **Drop onto Object** (Shift+G) sets the selection on the placeable or door below it. **Preview** is on a blueprint's row in the module tree and on a placed object's menu in the area and the tree. **Test From Here, Choose Character** joins Test From Here. Painting placeables, and placing onto a placeable as it is placed, are in the deferred list.

## 2026-10-13
* **Add**: [NUI Creator](manual/18-nui-creator.md), a chapter of its own, with [options](manual/10-options.md) (its keys), [the command line](manual/11-command-line.md) (`mg nui new`, `validate` and `generate`), [the plan](PLAN.md) (the crate `mg-nui`) and [the deferred list](deferred.md): Enhanced Edition UI windows (JUI) designed and previewed in the game's skin and fonts, checked against the stock `nw_inc_nui` API and what the game client does (NWN EE 8193.37), and built into an opener include and a compiled event script whose handlers are sections of code the builder writes; a contributor's pull request, brought in line with the codebase in review. Its frames on a 100-control window went from 26–35 ms after an edit to 1.4–2.4 ms (`crates/mg-ui/tests/nui_perf.rs`): the skin, fonts and picture list are read when they change, the window is parsed when it changes, the preview's texts are kept between frames. A field is one Undo step; labels, headings, the name window (New NUI Window) and OK and Cancel are the toolset's; a window is renamed and deleted with its settings and scripts. `mg-nui` has typed errors, keeps what it does not know in the settings, and builds through the module's compile path (the external compiler, `.ndb`, the compile memory). Its tests are where the crates keep theirs, and a client test run by hand (`crates/mg-corpus-tests/tests/client_nui.rs`, 32 windows) holds the checks to the game: it found one difference, a draw list with nothing in it after a clipped one, which the checks now refuse as the client blanks it.

Out of Settled: "Ctrl+V refused on Windows", "Sharing key sets", and "Area transitions can't be selected visually" (to Questions asked, unanswered).
